; VAR0  = health
; VAR1  = facing as usual
; VAR2  = flags -- during walking: (01=don't cycle walk animation; 02=faster walk cycle)
; VAR3  = ???
; VAR4  = ???
; VAR5  = freeze timer (appear yellow and shake)
; VAR6  = frame for walk cycle
; VAR7  = ???
; VAR8  = ???
; VAR9  = ???
; $0784 = ???
; $0785 = timer for automatic state transition
; $0786 = flags (02 set when retreating)
; $0787 = ???
; $0788 = ???
; $0789 = set to 1 during a state transition
; $078A = some kind of countdown
; $078B = iframes
;
; $0798 = X velocity backup
; $079A = Y velocity backup
; $079C = script sleep timer backup
; $079D = ???
; $079E = X position backup
; $07A0 = X fraction backup
; $07A1 = Y position backup
; $07A3 = Y fraction backup
; $07A4 = pose backup
;
; $07A9 = mosaic CHR bankswitch index
